## Parser diagnostics

- Format fingerprint: Scheme Code;ISIN Div Payout/ ISIN Growth;ISIN Div Reinvestment;Scheme Name;Plan;Option;Net Asset Value;Date
- Distinct category header lines: 103
- Repeated scheme-code rows: 0
- Rejected rows: 0
- Parser shape is accepted for this discovery run only; production batch-failure semantics are not changed in C0.

## S2 MFapi metadata

A real history response for AMFI code 129008 returned `meta.scheme_category=Debt Scheme - Banking and PSU Fund`, `meta.scheme_type=Open Ended Schemes`, `meta.scheme_name`, `fund_house`, `isin_growth`, and `isin_div_reinvestment`. The response status was SUCCESS. Full response shape is in [mfapi-meta.sample.json](../tests/fixtures/mfapi-meta.sample.json).

## S3 AMFI TER metadata

The official TER response for Axis Mutual Fund, month 10-2026, includes `SchemeCat_Desc=Hybrid Schemes - Aggressive Hybrid Fund`, `Scheme_Name`, `NSDLSchemeCode`, Direct/Regular TER, and a `meta` page descriptor. It does not provide an AMFI Scheme Code in the sampled row. The request asked for pageSize 10000, but the response says pageSize 100, total 2494, pageCount 25; callers must request and collect all 25 pages to see the full AMC dataset. See [amfi-ter.sample.json](../tests/fixtures/amfi-ter.sample.json).

## Prompt assumption check

- Confirmed from raw AMFI strings: `Life Cycle Funds` is present; Sectoral and Thematic occur as separate categories and as an older combined category; plural Equity/Hybrid families and legacy `Income/Debt Oriented Schemes` coexist.
- The Sectoral Debt examples are filed under `Income/Debt Oriented Schemes - Sectoral Fund`; the feed does not use the literal category string `Sectoral Debt Fund` in the observed set.
- Header-only `Growth`, `Income`, `Gilt`, and `ELSS` also occur. `Growth` and `Income` do not identify an asset class and remain `other / UNMAPPED` in the draft.
- The SEBI landing page confirms the stated circular title/date/number. Its linked PDF body could not be extracted during C0, so the detailed policy summary and the inferred August 2026 compliance deadline remain unverified.
- Passive-name outcomes are candidate rules only; ambiguous and unmatched names remain quarantined as `other` pending C2 review.
